\displaystyle II\leq\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

For any
j\geq 0
and
z=1,2,\cdots,T-\lambda_{1},
define

\displaystyle D_{z,j}=\sum_{t_{1}=T-z+1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right)

and
\mathcal{D}_{z,j}
the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},\cdots,e_{T-z+1-j}.
Then
D_{z,j}
is measurable in
\mathcal{D}_{z,j},

\mathcal{D}_{z,j}\subset\mathcal{D}_{z+1,j},
and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[D_{z+1,j}-D_{z,j}\mid\mathcal{D}_{z,j}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{2}=(T-z-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{T-z-\lambda_{1}}\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{T-z,T-z-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{(T-z)t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-z,j}\right]\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{T-z,T-z-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{(T-z)t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-z,j-1}\right]\right)\mid\mathcal{D}_{z,j}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{2}=(T-z-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{T-z-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{T-z,T-z-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{(T-z)t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-z,j-1}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{T-z,T-z-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{(T-z)t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-z,j-1}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=0,

so
D_{z,j}
forms a martingale. From Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al. 1972
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}.

Notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

On the one hand, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right\|_{M/2}.

Since

\displaystyle\left|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right|\leq\left|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right|_{2}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}\left|\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right|_{2},

from Cauchy–Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\left|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right|_{2}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}\left|\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right|_{2}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}\left\|\ \left|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M}\left\|\ \left|\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M}.

From (
3.2
)

\displaystyle\left\|\ \left|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M}\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}^{(i)}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}^{(i)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right]\right\|^{2}_{M}}\leq\frac{C\sqrt{d}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}},

we also have

\displaystyle\left\|\ \left|\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M}\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}^{(i)}\right\|^{2}_{M}}\leq C\sqrt{d},

which makes

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{Cda^{\dagger}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}},

(
\thechapter
.D.5)

where
a^{\dagger}=\max_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1},\cdots,T;\ t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1,\cdots,t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}|_{2}.

On the other hand, if
t_{1}-t_{2}<j,
we have

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}=\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}-\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},j-1}+\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},j-1}-\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}=\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},j-1}+\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},j-1}-\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right),

and
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},j-1}-\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}
is measurable in
\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},j-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},j-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},j-1}-\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+j-t_{1}}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+j-1-t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},j-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}\left\|\ \left|\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},j-1}-\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M}\left\|\ \left|\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+j-t_{1}}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+j-1-t_{1}}\right]\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M}.

We notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},j-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{Cda^{\dagger}}{j^{\alpha}}.

On the other hand,

\displaystyle\left\|\ \left|\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},j-1}-\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M}

\displaystyle=\left\|\ \left|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}-\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},j-1}\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M}

(
\thechapter
.D.6)

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\ \left|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M}+\left\|\ \left|\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},j-1}\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sqrt{d}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C\sqrt{d}}{j^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{2C\sqrt{d}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\ \left|\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+j-t_{1}}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+j-1-t_{1}}\right]\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}^{(i)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+j-t_{1}}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}^{(i)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+j-1-t_{1}}\right]\right\|^{2}_{M}}\leq\sqrt{d}\delta_{t_{2}+j-t_{1}},

so

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{Cda^{\dagger}}{j^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cda^{\dagger}\delta_{t_{2}+j-t_{1}}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}.

If
j\leq\lambda_{2},
then

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\frac{Cda^{\dagger}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{1}da^{\dagger}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha-1}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cda^{\dagger}\sqrt{T}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha-1}}.

If
j>\lambda_{2},
then for any
t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1,\cdots,t_{1}-\lambda_{1},

t_{1}-t_{2}<j,
so

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\frac{Cda^{\dagger}}{j^{\alpha}}+\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\frac{Cda^{\dagger}\delta_{t_{2}+j-t_{1}}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}da^{\dagger}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}{j^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cda^{\dagger}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha}}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\delta_{t_{2}+j-t_{1}}\leq\frac{C_{1}da^{\dagger}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}{j^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}da^{\dagger}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha}(j-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(
\thechapter
.D.7)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cda^{\dagger}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})\sqrt{T}}{j^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cda^{\dagger}\sqrt{T}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha}(j-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}.

From these observations, we have

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{\lambda_{2}}\frac{Cda^{\dagger}\sqrt{T}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha-1}}+\sum_{j=\lambda_{2}+1}^{\infty}\frac{Cda^{\dagger}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})\sqrt{T}}{j^{\alpha}}+\sum_{j=\lambda_{2}+1}^{\infty}\frac{Cda^{\dagger}\sqrt{T}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha}(j-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}

(
\thechapter
.D.8)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}da^{\dagger}\sqrt{T}\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{Cda^{\dagger}\sqrt{T}}{\lambda_{2}^{\alpha-2}}+\frac{C_{1}da^{\dagger}\sqrt{T}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{2}da^{\dagger}\sqrt{T}\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha-1}}.

For
d=O(T)
and
\kappa_{1}>\frac{5}{2(\alpha-2)},
we have

\displaystyle\frac{da^{\dagger}\sqrt{T}\lambda_{2}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha-1}}\leq\frac{CTa^{\dagger}T\sqrt{T}}{T^{5/2}}=Ca^{\dagger}=O\left(\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|^{2}_{2}}\right).

From (
\thechapter.D.4
) and (
\thechapter.D.8
), we prove (
3.3
).

For
S>2\lambda_{2}
and
t_{1}-t_{2}\leq\lambda_{2}<S,
define
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}
and
\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}
as in (
\thechapter.D.1
) with
s=t_{1}-t_{2}-1.
Then
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}
is measurable in
\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S},
and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]=\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+S-t_{1}}\right]+\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right],

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+S-t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

Since

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+S-t_{1}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+\lambda_{1}}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+S-t_{1}}\right]\right),

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+\lambda_{1}}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+S-t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+\lambda_{1}}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\sum_{j=t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1}^{\infty}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=S+1-\lambda_{2}}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})\vee(t_{2}+S+1-j)}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

For any
z=1,\cdots,T-\lambda_{1}
and any
j\geq S+1-\lambda_{2},
define

\displaystyle H_{z,j}=\sum_{t_{2}=T-\lambda_{1}+1-z}^{T-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})\vee(t_{2}+S+1-j)}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]\right)

and
\mathcal{H}_{z,j}
the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},e_{T-1},\cdots,e_{T-\lambda_{1}+1-z-j}.
Then
\mathcal{H}_{z,j}\subset\mathcal{H}_{z+1,j},

H_{z,j}
is measurable in
\mathcal{H}_{z,j},
and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[H_{z+1,j}-H_{z,j}\mid\mathcal{H}_{z,j}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{1}=(T-\lambda_{1}-z+\lambda_{1})\vee(T-\lambda_{1}-z+S+1-j)}^{(T-\lambda_{1}-z+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-T+\lambda_{1}+z-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}(T-\lambda_{1}-z)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{T-\lambda_{1}-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-\lambda_{1}-z,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{T-\lambda_{1}-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-\lambda_{1}-z,j-1}\right]\right)\mid\mathcal{H}_{z,j}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t_{1}=(T-\lambda_{1}-z+\lambda_{1})\vee(T-\lambda_{1}-z+S+1-j)}^{(T-\lambda_{1}-z+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-T+\lambda_{1}+z-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}(T-\lambda_{1}-z)}

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{T-\lambda_{1}-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-\lambda_{1}-z,j-1}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{T-\lambda_{1}-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-\lambda_{1}-z,j-1}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=0,

so
H_{z,j}
forms a martingale. From Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al. 1972
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})\vee(t_{2}+S+1-j)}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-\lambda_{1}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})\vee(t_{2}+S+1-j)}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}.

From (
\thechapter.D.2
) and Examples 1.22 of
Çınlar 2011
, for any vector
\mathbf{a}\in\mathbf{R}^{d}

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})\vee(t_{2}+S+1-j)}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]\right)\right|^{M/2}\right.

\displaystyle\mid\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]=\mathbf{a}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})\vee(t_{2}+S+1-j)}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{a}\right|^{M/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\mathbf{a}\right|_{2}^{M/2}\left(\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})\vee(t_{2}+S+1-j)}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}^{2}\right)^{M/4},

which makes

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})\vee(t_{2}+S+1-j)}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})\vee(t_{2}+S+1-j)}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}^{2}}\ \left\|\ \left|\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M/2}.

Since

\displaystyle\left\|\ \left|\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{d}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}^{(i)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}^{(i)}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}^{2}}\leq\sqrt{d}\delta_{j},

we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})\vee(t_{2}+S+1-j)}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\delta_{j}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})\vee(t_{2}+S+1-j)}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}^{2}}\leq C\delta_{j}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}^{2}},

which makes

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{t_{1}=(t_{2}+\lambda_{1})\vee(t_{2}+S+1-j)}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\delta_{j}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}^{2}}.

Since
d=O(T)
and
S>\lambda_{2},
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{t_{1}=t_{2}+\lambda_{1}}^{(t_{2}+\lambda_{2})\wedge T}\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2},t_{2}+S-t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(
\thechapter
.D.9)

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{j=S+1-\lambda_{2}}^{\infty}\delta_{j}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{(S+1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}^{2}}\leq\frac{C_{2}a^{\dagger}T\sqrt{\lambda_{2}}}{(S-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{3}a^{\dagger}T}{(S-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-1}}

On the other hand, notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=S+1}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

For
j\geq S+1>\lambda_{2},
from (
\thechapter.D.7
),

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cda^{\dagger}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})\sqrt{T}}{j^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cda^{\dagger}\sqrt{T}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha}(j-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}},

also notice that
S>\lambda_{2},

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1},t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(
\thechapter
.D.10)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=S+1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{Cda^{\dagger}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})\sqrt{T}}{j^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cda^{\dagger}\sqrt{T}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha}(j-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}da^{\dagger}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})\sqrt{T}}{(S+1)^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C_{1}da^{\dagger}\sqrt{T}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha}(S-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-1}}\leq\frac{C_{2}T^{3/2}a^{\dagger}}{(S+1)^{\alpha-2}}.

From (
\thechapter.D.9
) and (
\thechapter.D.10
), we prove (
3.4
).
∎

Remark 8
.

To make a further illustration of the bounds in equations (
3.2
) and (
3.3
), suppose
\mathbf{z}_{t},t\in\mathbf{Z}
are mutually independent with independent entries, i.e.,

\mathbf{z}_{t}^{(i)},t\in\mathbf{Z},i=1,\cdots,d
are mutually independent. In this case, from Theorem 2 of
Whittle 1960
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{z}_{t}\right\|_{M}=\left\|\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathbf{a}_{t}^{(i)}\mathbf{z}_{t}^{(i)}\right\|_{M}\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{d}\left|\mathbf{a}_{t}^{(i)}\right|^{2}}=C\sqrt{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left|\mathbf{a}_{t}\right|_{2}^{2}},

which is exactly the moment bound in equation (
3.2
). Furthermore, define the random vector
\boldsymbol{z}\in\mathbf{R}^{Td}
and the matrix
\boldsymbol{B}\in\mathbf{R}^{Td\times Td}
as follows:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{z}=\left[\begin{matrix}\mathbf{z}_{1}\\
\mathbf{z}_{2}\\
\vdots\\
\mathbf{z}_{T}\end{matrix}\right],\hskip 10.00002pt\boldsymbol{B}=\left[\begin{matrix}\boldsymbol{B}_{11}&\boldsymbol{B}_{12}&\cdots&\boldsymbol{B}_{1T}\\
\boldsymbol{B}_{21}&\boldsymbol{B}_{22}&\cdots&\boldsymbol{B}_{2T}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\cdots&\vdots\\
\boldsymbol{B}_{T1}&\boldsymbol{B}_{T2}&\cdots&\boldsymbol{B}_{TT}\end{matrix}\right],

where
\boldsymbol{B}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\in\mathbf{R}^{d\times d}
is defined as follows:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{B}_{t_{1}t_{2}}=\begin{cases}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}&\text{if}\quad t_{1}\geq 1+\lambda_{1}\quad\text{and}\quad(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1\leq t_{2}\leq t_{1}-\lambda_{1},\\
0&\text{Otherwise}.\end{cases}

Then

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{B}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}\boldsymbol{B}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\boldsymbol{z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{B}\boldsymbol{z}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{B}\boldsymbol{z}\right]\right\|_{M/2}.

From Theorem 2 of
Whittle 1960
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{B}\boldsymbol{z}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{z}^{\top}\boldsymbol{B}\boldsymbol{z}\right]\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\left|\boldsymbol{B}\right|_{F}=C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1}^{T}\left|\boldsymbol{B}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{F}^{2}}=C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{F}^{2}}.

We derive the moment bound in (
3.3
) by further employing the inequality
\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{F}\leq\sqrt{d}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}.
As a result, we adopt a relatively loose moment bound to compensate for the effect of temporal dependence in the proof. However, the inequality
\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{F}\leq\sqrt{d}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}
does not change the order of the moment bound: If all singular values of
\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}

are of the same order, then
\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{F}\asymp\sqrt{d}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}.
Since our primary interest lies in the order of the moment bound, replacing
\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{F}
with
\sqrt{d}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|_{2}
does not lead to too much information loss.

We then present a corollary of Theorem
3.1
that serves an important role in the proof of Theorem
3.4
.

Corollary
\thechapter
.D.1
.

Suppose the conditions of Theorem
3.1
hold true and suppose
h\in\mathbf{Z}
satisfies
h=O(T^{\kappa_{h}})
with
0<\kappa_{h}<1.
Then there exists a constant
C>0
such that for any
t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1},\cdots,T,

1\vee(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\leq t_{2}\leq t_{1}-\lambda_{1},
and
any real symmetric matrices
\mathbf{B}_{u},u=0,1,\cdots,h,
we have

\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left|\mathbf{B}_{u}\right|^{2}_{2}}.

(
\thechapter
.D.11)

Furthermore, for any
S>2\lambda_{2}+h,

\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C\sqrt{dh}b^{\dagger}}{(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}},

(
\thechapter
.D.12)

where the notations
\lambda_{1},\lambda_{2}
coincide with Theorem
3.1
, and
b^{\dagger}=\max_{u=0,\cdots,h}|\mathbf{B}_{u}|_{2}.

Proof of Corollary
\thechapter.D.1
.

For given
t_{1}
and
t_{2},
we define the matrices
\mathbf{A}_{t_{3}t_{4}}
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbf{A}_{t_{3}t_{4}}=\begin{cases}\mathbf{B}_{u}&\text{if}\quad t_{3}=t_{1}-u\quad\text{and}\quad t_{4}=t_{2}-u,\quad\text{where}\quad u=0,1,\cdots,h\wedge(t_{2}-1),\\
0&\text{Otherwise.}\end{cases}

In such case,

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{3}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{4}=(t_{3}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{3}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{3}}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{3}t_{4}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{4}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{3}}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{3}t_{4}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{4}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\right]\right),

so from equation (
3.3
),

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{3}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{4}=(t_{3}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{3}-\lambda_{1}}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{3}t_{4}}\right|^{2}_{2}}

\displaystyle=C\sqrt{d\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left|\mathbf{B}_{u}\right|^{2}_{2}},

which proves (
\thechapter.D.11
).

Consider the decomposition
\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}=\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}+\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1},
where
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}
and
\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}
coincide with (
\thechapter.D.1
) with
s=t_{1}-t_{2}-1.
Then
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}
is measurable in
\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S},
and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,t_{2}+S-u-t_{1}}\right]\right)\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}.

For

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,t_{2}+S-u-t_{1}}\right]\right)\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\sum_{j=t_{2}+S-u-t_{1}+1}^{\infty}\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j-1}\right]\right)\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=t_{2}+S-h-t_{1}+1}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{u=0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j}\right]\right.\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j-1}\right]\right)\right)\Bigg\|_{M/2}.

For any
j=t_{2}+S-h-t_{1}+1,\cdots,
and any
z=1,\cdots,h\wedge(t_{2}-1)-0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)+1,
define

\displaystyle J_{z,j}=\sum_{u=0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)}^{0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)+z-1}\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j-1}\right]\right)\right)

and
\mathcal{J}_{z,j}
the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},e_{T-1},\cdots,e_{t_{2}-0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)-z+1-j}.
Then
J_{z,j}
is measurable in
\mathcal{J}_{z,j},

\mathcal{J}_{z,j}\subset\mathcal{J}_{z+1,j},
and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[J_{z+1,j}-J_{z,j}\mid\mathcal{J}_{z,j}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)-z,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)+z}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)-z,j}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\left.-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)-z,j-1}\right]\right)\right)\mid\mathcal{J}_{z,j}\right]

\displaystyle=\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)-z,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)+z}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)-z,j-1}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)-z,j-1}\right]\right)=0.

Therefore,
J_{z,j}
forms a martingale, and from Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al. 1972
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{u=0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j-1}\right]\right)\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{u=0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left\|\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j-1}\right]\right)\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}.

For any vector
\mathbf{a}\in\mathbf{R}^{d},

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{a}\right\|_{M/2}=\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{a}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}\leq\left\|\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{a}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\left|\mathbf{B}_{u}\right|_{2}|\mathbf{a}|_{2}.

Since
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}
is independent of
\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j-1}\right],
from Examples 1.22 of
Çınlar 2011
, for any
\mathbf{a}\in\mathbf{R}^{d},

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j-1}\right]\right)\right|^{M/2}\right.

\displaystyle\mid\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j-1}\right]=\mathbf{a}\Big]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{a}\right|^{M/2}\right]\leq C\left|\mathbf{B}_{u}\right|_{2}^{M/2}|\mathbf{a}|_{2}^{M/2},

which implies

\displaystyle\left\|\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C|\mathbf{B}_{u}|_{2}\left\|\ \left|\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j-1}\right]\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M/2}\leq C_{1}|\mathbf{B}_{u}|_{2}\delta_{j}\sqrt{d},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{u=0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,j-1}\right]\right)\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\delta_{j}\sqrt{d\sum_{u=0\vee(t_{2}+S-t_{1}+1-j)}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}|\mathbf{B}_{u}|^{2}_{2}}\leq C\delta_{j}\sqrt{d\sum_{u=0}^{h}|\mathbf{B}_{u}|^{2}_{2}},

which implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,t_{2}+S-u-t_{1}}\right]\right)\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(
\thechapter
.D.13)

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{j=t_{2}+S-h-t_{1}+1}^{\infty}\delta_{j}\sqrt{d\sum_{u=0}^{h}|\mathbf{B}_{u}|^{2}_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{(t_{2}+S-h-t_{1}+1)^{\alpha}}\sqrt{d\sum_{u=0}^{h}|\mathbf{B}_{u}|^{2}_{2}}\leq\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{dh}b^{\dagger}}{(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}.

On the other hand, since

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,S-u}\right],

we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\sum_{j=S-u+1}^{\infty}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=S-h+1}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{u=0\vee(S-j+1)}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j}\right]\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}\right]\right)\Bigg\|_{M/2}

For any
j=S-h+1,\cdots,
and
z=1,\cdots,h\wedge(t_{2}-1)-0\vee(S-j+1)+1,
define

\displaystyle K_{z,j}=\sum_{u=0\vee(S-j+1)}^{0\vee(S-j+1)+z-1}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}\right]\right)

and
\mathcal{K}_{z,j}
the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},\cdots,e_{t_{1}-j-0\vee(S-j+1)-z+1}.
Then
K_{z,j}
is measurable in
\mathcal{K}_{z,j},

\mathcal{K}_{z,j}\subset\mathcal{K}_{z+1,j},
and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[K_{z+1,j}-K_{z,j}\mid\mathcal{K}_{z,j}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-0\vee(S-j+1)-z,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{0\vee(S-j+1)+z}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-0\vee(S-j+1)-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-0\vee(S-j+1)-z,j}\right]\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-0\vee(S-j+1)-z,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{0\vee(S-j+1)+z}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-0\vee(S-j+1)-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-0\vee(S-j+1)-z,j-1}\right]\right)\mid\mathcal{K}_{z,j}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-0\vee(S-j+1)-z,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{0\vee(S-j+1)+z}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-0\vee(S-j+1)-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-0\vee(S-j+1)-z,j-1}\right]

\displaystyle-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-0\vee(S-j+1)-z,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{0\vee(S-j+1)+z}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-0\vee(S-j+1)-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-0\vee(S-j+1)-z,j-1}\right]=0,

so
K_{z,j}
forms a martingale. From Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al. 1972
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{u=0\vee(S-j+1)}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{u=0\vee(S-j+1)}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}\right]\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}

Since
j\geq S-h+1>t_{1}-t_{2},
we can establish the following decomposition

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}=\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}+\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}-\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1},

and
\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}-\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}
is measurable in
\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}.
In such case,

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}-\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,t_{2}+j-t_{1}}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,t_{2}+j-t_{1}-1}\right]\right),

and from (
\thechapter.D.6
),

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}-\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,t_{2}+j-t_{1}}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,t_{2}+j-t_{1}-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq|\mathbf{B}_{u}|_{2}\left\|\ \left|\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}-\boldsymbol{\zeta}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}\right|_{2}\right\|_{M}\left\|\ \left|(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,t_{2}+j-t_{1}}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{2}-u,t_{2}+j-t_{1}-1}\right]\right|_{2}\ \right\|_{M}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{Cb^{\dagger}d}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\delta_{t_{2}+j-t_{1}}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{u=0\vee(S-j+1)}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}-u,j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{u=0\vee(S-j+1)}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\frac{Cb^{\dagger}d}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\delta_{t_{2}+j-t_{1}}\right)^{2}}\leq\frac{Cb^{\dagger}d\sqrt{h}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\delta_{t_{2}+j-t_{1}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\boldsymbol{\omega}_{t_{1}-u,t_{1}-t_{2}-1}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(
\thechapter
.D.14)

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{j=S-h+1}^{\infty}\frac{b^{\dagger}d\sqrt{h}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\delta_{t_{2}+j-t_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}b^{\dagger}d\sqrt{h}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{(S-h+1+t_{2}-t_{1})^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{1}b^{\dagger}d\sqrt{h}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}.

From (
\thechapter.D.13
) and (
\thechapter.D.14
),

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sqrt{dh}b^{\dagger}}{(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cb^{\dagger}d\sqrt{h}}{(t_{1}-t_{2})^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sqrt{dh}b^{\dagger}}{(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{Cb^{\dagger}d\sqrt{h}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha}}\frac{1}{(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{2C\sqrt{dh}b^{\dagger}}{(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}},

which proves (
\thechapter.D.12
).
∎

Corollary
\thechapter
.D.2
.

Suppose conditions of Corollary
\thechapter.D.1
hold true. Then for any
S>2\lambda_{2}+h,
we further derive

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|^{2}_{2}}+\frac{CT\sqrt{T}a^{\dagger}}{(S-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{d\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=(t_{1}-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t_{1}-\lambda_{1}}\left|\mathbf{A}_{t_{1}t_{2}}\right|^{2}_{2}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-u}^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{u}\mathbf{z}_{t_{2}-u}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left|\mathbf{B}_{u}\right|^{2}_{2}}+\frac{C\sqrt{dh}b^{\dagger}}{(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{1}\sqrt{d\sum_{u=0}^{h\wedge(t_{2}-1)}\left|\mathbf{B}_{u}\right|^{2}_{2}}.

\thechapter
.D.1
Technical details of distributional results under
H_{0}

Some technical assumptions are needed for the distributional results of the test statistic, which are summarized as follows:

Assumption
\thechapter
.D.1
.

Suppose independent random variables
e_{t}=(e_{t}^{(1)},e_{t}^{(2)})\in\mathbf{H}^{(1)}\times\mathbf{H}^{(2)}
for measurable spaces
\mathbf{H}^{(1)},\mathbf{H}^{(2)}.
Furthermore, assume that
e_{t}^{(1)}
is independent of
e_{t}^{(2)}
for any
t\in\mathbf{Z},
and

\displaystyle\mathbf{z}_{t}=\left(\begin{matrix}\mathbf{x}_{t}\\
\mathbf{y}_{t}\end{matrix}\right)=\left(\begin{matrix}g_{t,T}^{(1)}\left(\cdots,e_{t-2}^{(1)},e_{t-1}^{(1)},e_{t}^{(1)}\right)\\
g_{t,T}^{(2)}\left(\cdots,e_{t-2}^{(2)},e_{t-1}^{(2)},e_{t}^{(2)}\right)\end{matrix}\right),

where
g_{t,T}^{(1)}\left(\cdot\right):\mathbf{H}^{(1)\mathbf{Z}}\to\mathbf{R}^{d_{1}}
and
g_{t,T}^{(2)}\left(\cdot\right):\mathbf{H}^{(2)\mathbf{Z}}\to\mathbf{R}^{d_{2}}
are respectively measurable functions in
\mathbf{H}^{(1)\mathbf{Z}}
and
\mathbf{H}^{(2)\mathbf{Z}}.
Assume that
\mathbf{z}_{t},t\in\mathbf{Z}
satisfy Definition
2
with
M,\alpha>8,
and
d_{1},d_{2}\asymp T.

Assumption
\thechapter.D.1
further restricts the functional form of
g_{t,T}(\cdot)
in (
3.1
). Specifically, it requires that
\mathbf{x}_{t}
,
t\in\mathbf{Z}
, depend only on
{e_{t}^{(1)},t\in\mathbf{Z}}
, while
\mathbf{y}_{t}
,
t\in\mathbf{Z}
, depend only on
{e_{t}^{(2)},t\in\mathbf{Z}}
. Since
e_{t}^{(1)}
and
e_{t}^{(2)}
are independent for all
t
, Assumption
\thechapter.D.1
guarantees that the sequence
\{\mathbf{x}_{t},t\in\mathbf{Z}\}
is independent of the sequence
\{\mathbf{y}_{t},t\in\mathbf{Z}\}
.

Assumption
\thechapter
.D.2
.

Suppose the parameters satisfy
\lambda_{1}\asymp T^{\kappa_{1}},

\lambda_{2}\asymp T^{\kappa_{2}},
and
h\asymp T^{\kappa_{h}},
where
1>\kappa_{2}>\kappa_{1}>\frac{5}{2(\alpha-2)}
and
0<\kappa_{h}<1.

Assumption
\thechapter
.D.3
.

Define the canonical statistic

\displaystyle\widehat{R}^{\dagger}=\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}O_{t}^{\dagger},\hskip 10.00002ptO_{t}^{\dagger}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=1}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\right]\right).

Assume that there exists a constant
c>0
such that
\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)>c
for sufficiently large
T.

We call
\widehat{R}^{\dagger}
“canonical” because it cannot be calculated in practice, as the expectations
\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]
and
\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\right]
are unknown and hard to estimate, especially in the setting of nonstationary time series, whose autocovariances may change with the time index
t.
Suppose Assumptions
\thechapter.D.1
and
\thechapter.D.2
hold true. For each
t,t_{1},
define

\gamma_{tt_{1}}=\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right),

(
\thechapter
.D.15)

and define the matrices
\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\in\mathbf{R}^{d\times d},
where
\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}^{(i,i)}=1
for
i=1,\cdots,d_{1},
and
\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}^{(i,j)}=0
otherwise; and
\mathbf{I}_{d_{2}}\in\mathbf{R}^{d\times d},
where
\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}^{(i,i)}=1
for
i=d_{1}+1,\cdots,d,
and
\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}^{(i,j)}=0
otherwise. With these notations, we have
\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}=\mathbf{z}^{\top}_{t}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{t}
and
\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}=\mathbf{z}^{\top}_{t}\mathbf{I}_{d_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t}.
Furthermore, from Corollary
\thechapter.D.1
,

\displaystyle\left\|\gamma_{tt_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{I}_{d_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{I}_{d_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(
\thechapter
.D.16)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left|\mathbf{I}_{d_{2}}\right|^{2}_{2}}\leq C\sqrt{dh}

Since the random variables
\left\{\gamma_{tt_{1}}:t=1+\lambda_{1},\cdots,T,t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1,\cdots,t-\lambda_{1}\right\}
are independent of
\mathbf{x}_{t},t\in\mathbf{Z},
from Examples 1.22 of
Çınlar 2011
and Theorem
3.1
, for any real numbers
c_{tt_{1}},

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\gamma_{tt_{1}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right|^{M/2}\right.

\displaystyle\mid\gamma_{tt_{1}}=c_{tt_{1}},t=1+\lambda_{1},\cdots,T,t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1,\cdots,t-\lambda_{1}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}c_{tt_{1}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right|^{M/2}\right]\leq C\left(\sqrt{d\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}c^{2}_{tt_{1}}}\right)^{M/2}.

Therefore, from Corollary
\thechapter.D.1
and (
\thechapter.D.16
),

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\gamma_{tt_{1}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(
\thechapter
.D.17)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d}\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\gamma^{2}_{tt_{1}}}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{d\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\left\|\gamma_{tt_{1}}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}\leq C_{1}d\sqrt{T(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})h}.

Similar to (
\thechapter.D.15
) to (
\thechapter.D.17
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=1}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq Cd\sqrt{T(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})h},

and

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)\leq\left\|\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}^{2}\leq C.

(
\thechapter
.D.18)

Therefore, Assumption
\thechapter.D.3
actually ensures that
\widehat{R}^{\dagger}
has a constant order. Furthermore, as demonstrated in the proof of Theorem
3.3
, the difference between
\widehat{R}
and
\widehat{R}^{\dagger}
is negligible asymptotically, so under
H_{0},

\widehat{R}
should also have a constant order.

As in Section
\thechapter.D
, we denote
\mathcal{F}_{t,S}
as the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{t-S},e_{t-S+1},\ldots,e_{t}
, where
e_{t}=(e_{t}^{(1)},e_{t}^{(2)})
.

Proof of Theorem
3.3
.

Define the functions

\displaystyle g_{0}(u)=\left(1-\min\left(1,\max(u,0)\right)^{4}\right)^{4}\hskip 10.00002pt\text{and}\hskip 10.00002ptg_{\psi,t}(u)=g_{0}\left(\psi(u-t)\right).

According to (S2) - (S5) of
Xu et al. 2019
, we have

\displaystyle g_{*}=\sup_{u\in\mathbf{R}}\left(\left|g^{\prime}_{0}(u)\right|+\left|g^{\prime\prime}_{0}(u)\right|+\left|g^{\prime\prime\prime}_{0}(u)\right|\right)<\infty,\hskip 10.00002pt\mathbf{1}_{u\leq t}\leq g_{\psi,t}(u)\leq\mathbf{1}_{u\leq t+\psi^{-1}},

\displaystyle\text{and}\hskip 10.00002pt\sup_{x,t\in\mathbf{R}}\left|g_{\psi,t}^{\prime}(u)\right|\leq g_{*}\psi,\hskip 10.00002pt\sup_{x,t\in\mathbf{R}}\left|g_{\psi,t}^{\prime\prime}(u)\right|\leq g_{*}\psi^{2},\hskip 10.00002pt\sup_{x,t\in\mathbf{R}}\left|g_{\psi,t}^{\prime\prime\prime}(u)\right|\leq g_{*}\psi^{3}.

Since
\mathrm{Var}(\epsilon)>c>0,
for any given
\psi>0,

\displaystyle\mathbf{pr}\left(\widehat{R}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{pr}\left(\widehat{R}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x+\frac{1}{\psi}\right)+\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x+\frac{1}{\psi}\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{R}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\epsilon\right)\right]+\int_{t\in[x,x+1/\psi]}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\mathrm{Var}(\epsilon)}}\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2\mathrm{Var}(\epsilon)}\right)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{R}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\epsilon\right)\right]\right|+\frac{C}{\psi},

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{pr}\left(\widehat{R}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{pr}\left(\widehat{R}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x-\frac{1}{\psi}\right)-\left(\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x-\frac{1}{\psi}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x-1/\psi}\left(\widehat{R}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x-1/\psi}\left(\epsilon\right)\right]-\int_{t\in[x-1/\psi,x]}\frac{1}{\sqrt{2\pi\mathrm{Var}(\epsilon)}}\exp\left(-\frac{t^{2}}{2\mathrm{Var}(\epsilon)}\right)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\geq-\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{R}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\epsilon\right)\right]\right|-\frac{C}{\psi},

so we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{pr}\left(\widehat{R}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x\right)\right|\leq\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{R}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\epsilon\right)\right]\right|+\frac{C}{\psi}.

(
\thechapter
.D.19)

Since

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq I+II+III,

where

\displaystyle I=\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\left(\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2},

\displaystyle II=\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2},

\displaystyle III=\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right|\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right|.

From (
\thechapter.B.2
) and (
\thechapter.D.16
),

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right|\left\|\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{dT(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}}\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\frac{d_{1}}{(t-t_{1})^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{1}T}{\sqrt{\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1}}\lambda_{1}^{\alpha-1}}\leq\frac{C_{2}}{T^{3/2}}.

From Theorem
3.1
,

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\left(\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\mathcal{U}}\sqrt{d\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\left(\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)^{2}}.

From (
\thechapter.B.2
),

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right|\leq\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\frac{Cd_{2}}{(t-t_{1})^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{1}dh}{(t-t_{1})^{\alpha}},

so

\displaystyle II\leq\frac{Cdh}{\mathcal{U}}\sqrt{d\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\frac{1}{(t-t_{1})^{2\alpha}}}\leq\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{Th}}{\lambda_{1}^{\alpha-1/2}\sqrt{\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1}}}\leq\frac{C_{2}}{T^{3/2}}.

From (
\thechapter.B.2
),

\displaystyle III

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\frac{Cd^{2}}{(t-t_{1})^{2\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}d^{2}h}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\frac{1}{(t-t_{1})^{2\alpha}}\leq\frac{C_{2}d^{2}hT}{\mathcal{U}\lambda_{1}^{2\alpha-1}}\leq\frac{C_{3}}{T^{3}}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C}{T^{3/2}}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=1}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\right.

\displaystyle-\left.\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=1}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C}{T^{3/2}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{R}^{\dagger}-\widehat{R}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C}{T^{3/2}}.

For any given
\psi>0,
we have

\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{R}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)\right]\right|\leq C\psi\left\|\widehat{R}-\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C_{1}\psi}{T^{3/2}}.

(
\thechapter
.D.20)

Choose
S>2\lambda_{2}+h,
and define

\displaystyle\widehat{R}^{\dagger}_{S}

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}O^{\dagger}_{t,S},

(
\thechapter
.D.21)

\displaystyle O^{\dagger}_{t,S}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=1}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\right]\right).

Define

\gamma_{tt_{1},S}=\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right),

(
\thechapter
.D.22)

then from Corollary
\thechapter.D.1
,

\displaystyle\left\|\gamma_{tt_{1}}-\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\right\|_{M/2}=\left\|\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{I}_{d_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{I}_{d_{2}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}-s}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C\sqrt{dh}}{(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}},

and from (
\thechapter.D.16
),

\displaystyle\left\|\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\left\|\gamma_{tt_{1}}\right\|_{M/2}+\left\|\gamma_{tt_{1}}-\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{dh}.

(
\thechapter
.D.23)

We also have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right),

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\gamma_{tt_{1}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\left(\gamma_{tt_{1}}-\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

From Assumption
\thechapter.D.1
,
\gamma_{tt_{1}}-\gamma_{tt_{1},S}
is a function of
\cdots,e_{t-2}^{(2)},e_{t-1}^{(2)},e_{t}^{(2)},
which makes it independent of
\left\{\mathbf{x}_{t},t\in\mathbf{Z}\right\}
under
H_{0}.
Therefore,
for any real numbers
c_{tt_{1}},t=1+\lambda_{1},\cdots,T,t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1,\cdots,t-\lambda_{1},
from Examples 1.22 of
Çınlar 2011
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\left(\gamma_{tt_{1}}-\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right|^{M/2}\mid\gamma_{tt_{1}}-\gamma_{tt_{1},S}=c_{tt_{1}},\right.

\displaystyle t=1+\lambda_{1},\cdots,T,t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1,\cdots,t-\lambda_{1}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}c_{tt_{1}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right|^{M/2}\right]\leq C\left(\sqrt{d\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}c_{tt_{1}}^{2}}\right)^{M/2},

which makes

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\left(\gamma_{tt_{1}}-\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\right)\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(
\thechapter
.D.24)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sqrt{d}}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sqrt{\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\left(\gamma_{tt_{1}}-\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\right)^{2}}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\sqrt{d}}{\mathcal{U}}\sqrt{\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\left\|\gamma_{tt_{1}}-\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}\leq\frac{C_{1}}{(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}.

Similarly, notice that
\gamma_{tt_{1},S}
is a function of
\cdots,e_{t-2}^{(2)},e_{t-1}^{(2)},e_{t}^{(2)}
and
\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]
is a function of
\cdots,e_{t-2}^{(1)},e_{t-1}^{(1)},e_{t}^{(1)}
for any
t,t_{1},
the sequence

\left\{\gamma_{tt_{1},S}:t=1+\lambda_{1},\cdots,T,\hskip 10.00002ptt_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1,\cdots,t-\lambda_{1}\right\}

is independent of the sequence

\left\{\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]:t=1+\lambda_{1},\cdots,T,\hskip 10.00002ptt_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1,\cdots,t-\lambda_{1}\right\},

and Theorem
3.1
implies

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]\right)\right)^{M/2}\right.

\displaystyle\mid\gamma_{tt_{1},S}=c_{tt_{1}},t=1+\lambda_{1},\cdots,T,t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1,\cdots,t-\lambda_{1}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}c_{tt_{1}}\left(\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{z}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{I}_{d_{1}}\mathbf{z}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]\right)\right)^{M/2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{CT\sqrt{T}}{(S-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}\max_{t=1+\lambda_{1},\cdots,T;\ t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1,\cdots,t-\lambda_{1}}\left|c_{tt_{1}}\right|\right)^{M/2},

and notice that
M>8,
which makes

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\gamma_{tt_{1}}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

(
\thechapter
.D.25)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CT\sqrt{T}}{\mathcal{U}(S-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}\left\|\max_{t=1+\lambda_{1},\cdots,T;\ t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1,\cdots,t-\lambda_{1}}\left|\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\right|\ \right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{d}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M-1/2}}{(S-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}\leq\frac{C_{2}T^{3/4}}{(S-\lambda_{2}-h)^{\alpha-2}}.

From equations (
\thechapter.D.24
), (
\thechapter.D.25
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CT^{3/4}}{(S-\lambda_{2}-h)^{\alpha-2}}.

Similarly, we can derive

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=1}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=1}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CT^{3/4}}{(S-\lambda_{2}-h)^{\alpha-2}},

which implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{R}^{\dagger}-\widehat{R}^{\dagger}_{S}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{CT^{3/4}}{(S-\lambda_{2}-h)^{\alpha-2}},

(
\thechapter
.D.26)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}_{S}\right)\right]\right|\leq\frac{C\psi T^{3/4}}{(S-\lambda_{2}-h)^{\alpha-2}}.

(
\thechapter
.D.27)

Choose
W>S
and define the “big-block”

\displaystyle A_{z}=\sum_{t=(1+\lambda_{1})\vee((z-1)(W+S)+1)}^{T\wedge((z-1)(W+S)+W)}O^{\dagger}_{t,S},\hskip 10.00002pta_{z}=\sum_{t=(1+\lambda_{1})\vee((z-1)(W+S)+W+1)}^{T\wedge(z(W+S))}O^{\dagger}_{t,S}.

Since
O^{\dagger}_{t,S}
is measurable in
\mathcal{F}_{t,S},

A_{z}
is measurable in the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T\wedge((z-1)(W+S)+W)},\cdots,e_{(1+\lambda_{1})\vee((z-1)(W+S)+1)-S},

A_{z}
are mutually independent. Similarly,

a_{z}
is measurable in the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T\wedge(z(W+S))},\cdots,e_{(1+\lambda_{1})\vee((z-1)(W+S)+W+1)-S},
so
a_{z}
are mutually independent. Furthermore, from Corollary
\thechapter.D.2
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t=(1+\lambda_{1})\vee((z-1)(W+S)+1)}^{T\wedge((z-1)(W+S)+W)}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\Bigg\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t=(1+\lambda_{1})\vee((z-1)(W+S)+1)}^{T\wedge((z-1)(W+S)+W)}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\mathcal{U}}\sqrt{d\sum_{t=(1+\lambda_{1})\vee((z-1)(W+S)+1)}^{T\wedge((z-1)(W+S)+W)}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\left\|\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{\frac{W}{T}},

similarly

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t=(1+\lambda_{1})\vee((z-1)(W+S)+1)}^{T\wedge((z-1)(W+S)+W)}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\Bigg\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\frac{W}{T}},

which makes

\displaystyle\left\|A_{z}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{\frac{W}{T}}.

(
\thechapter
.D.28)

Also from Corollary
\thechapter.D.2
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t=(1+\lambda_{1})\vee((z-1)(W+S)+W+1)}^{T\wedge(z(W+S))}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=0}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right.
