\displaystyle\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\Bigg\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\sum_{t=(1+\lambda_{1})\vee((z-1)(W+S)+W+1)}^{T\wedge(z(W+S))}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\mathcal{U}}\sqrt{d\sum_{t=(1+\lambda_{1})\vee((z-1)(W+S)+W+1)}^{T\wedge(z(W+S))}\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\left\|\gamma_{tt_{1},S}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{\frac{S}{T}},

which makes

\displaystyle\left\|a_{z}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{\frac{S}{T}}.

(
\thechapter
.D.29)

Define
P=\lceil\frac{T}{W+S}\rceil,
where
\lceil x\rceil
stands for the smallest integer that is larger than or equal to
x,
then

\displaystyle\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}+\sum_{z=1}^{P}a_{z}=\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}O^{\dagger}_{t,S},

so from Theorem 2 of
Whittle 1960
,

\displaystyle\left\|\widehat{R}^{\dagger}_{S}-\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}\right\|_{M/2}=\left\|\sum_{z=1}^{P}a_{z}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{z=1}^{P}\left\|a_{z}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}\leq C_{1}\sqrt{\frac{S}{W}},

(
\thechapter
.D.30)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}_{S}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}\right)\right]\right|\leq C\psi\left\|\widehat{R}^{\dagger}_{S}-\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\psi\sqrt{\frac{S}{W}}.

(
\thechapter
.D.31)

Define
A_{z}^{*}
as independent random variables with
\mathbf{E}\left[A_{z}^{*}\right]=0
and
\mathrm{Var}\left(A_{z}^{*}\right)=\mathrm{Var}\left(A_{z}\right),
and
A_{z}^{*},z=1,\cdots,P
are independent of
\mathbf{z}_{t},t\in\mathbf{Z}.
For any
u=1,\cdots,P,
define

\displaystyle B_{u}=\sum_{z=1}^{u-1}A_{z}+\sum_{z=u+1}^{P}A^{*}_{z},

then

\displaystyle B_{u}+A_{u}=B_{u+1}+A_{u+1}^{*}.

From Taylor’s expansion,

\displaystyle g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}+A_{u}\right)=g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}\right)+g_{\psi,x}^{\prime}\left(B_{u}\right)A_{u}+\frac{1}{2}g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime}\left(B_{u}\right)A_{u}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{B_{u}}^{B_{u}+A_{u}}g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime\prime}(t)\left(B_{u}+A_{u}-t\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}t,

and

\displaystyle g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}+A_{u}^{*}\right)=g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}\right)+g_{\psi,x}^{\prime}\left(B_{u}\right)A_{u}^{*}+\frac{1}{2}g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime}\left(B_{u}\right)A_{u}^{*2}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{B_{u}}^{B_{u}+A_{u}^{*}}g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime\prime}(t)\left(B_{u}+A^{*}_{u}-t\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}t.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}+A_{u}\right)-g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}+A_{u}^{*}\right)\mid B_{u}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|g_{\psi,x}^{\prime}\left(B_{u}\right)\left(\mathbf{E}\left[A_{u}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[A_{u}^{*}\right]\right)+\frac{1}{2}g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime}\left(B_{u}\right)\left(\mathrm{Var}\left(A_{u}\right)-\mathrm{Var}\left(A^{*}_{u}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\mathbf{E}\left[\frac{1}{2}\int_{B_{u}}^{B_{u}+A_{u}}g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime\prime}(t)\left(B_{u}+A_{u}-t\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}t\mid B_{u}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\frac{1}{2}\int_{B_{u}}^{B_{u}+A_{u}^{*}}g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime\prime}(t)\left(B_{u}+A^{*}_{u}-t\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}t\mid B_{u}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{E}\left[\int_{B_{u}}^{B_{u}+A_{u}}\left|g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime\prime}(t)\right|\left(B_{u}+A_{u}-t\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}t\mid B_{u}\right]+\frac{1}{2}\mathbf{E}\left[\int_{B_{u}}^{B_{u}+A^{*}_{u}}\left|g_{\psi,x}^{\prime\prime\prime}(t)\right|\left(B_{u}+A^{*}_{u}-t\right)^{2}\mathrm{d}t\mid B_{u}\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\psi^{3}\mathbf{E}\left[\left|A_{u}\right|^{3}\right]+C\psi^{3}\mathbf{E}\left[\left|A_{u}^{*}\right|^{3}\right]\leq C\psi^{3}\left\|A_{u}\right\|_{M/2}^{3}+C\psi^{3}\left\|A_{u}^{*}\right\|_{M/2}^{3}.

For
A^{*}_{u}
has normal distribution, there exists a constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle\left\|A_{u}^{*}\right\|_{M/2}\leq C\left\|A_{u}^{*}\right\|_{2}=C\left\|A_{u}\right\|_{2}\leq C\left\|A_{u}\right\|_{M/2},

so from (
\thechapter.D.28
),

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}+A_{u}\right)-g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}+A_{u}^{*}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbf{E}\left[\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}+A_{u}\right)-g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}+A_{u}^{*}\right)\mid B_{u}\right]\right|\right]

\displaystyle\leq C\psi^{3}\left(\frac{W}{T}\right)^{3/2}.

By taking summations, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}^{*}\right)\right]\right|

(
\thechapter
.D.32)

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{P}+A_{P}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{1}+A_{1}^{*}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{1}+A_{1}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{1}+A_{1}^{*}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{u=2}^{P}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}+A_{u}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u-1}+A_{u-1}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{u=1}^{P}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}+A_{u}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(B_{u}+A^{*}_{u}\right)\right]\right|\leq C\psi^{3}\left(\frac{W}{T}\right)^{3/2}P\leq C_{1}\psi^{3}\sqrt{\frac{W}{T}}.

Finally, since
A^{*}_{z}
are mutually independent,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}^{*}\right\|_{2}=\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}^{*}\right)}=\sqrt{\sum_{z=1}^{P}\mathrm{Var}\left(A_{z}^{*}\right)}=\sqrt{\sum_{z=1}^{P}\mathrm{Var}\left(A_{z}\right)}=\left\|\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}\right\|_{2},

so from (
\thechapter.D.26
), and (
\thechapter.D.30
),

\displaystyle\left|\ \left\|\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}^{*}\right\|_{2}-\left\|\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}\ \right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\ \left\|\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}\right\|_{2}-\left\|\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}+\sum_{z=1}^{P}a_{z}\right\|_{2}\ \right|+\left|\ \left\|\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}O^{\dagger}_{t,S}\right\|_{2}-\left\|\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}\ \right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{z=1}^{P}a_{z}\right\|_{M/2}+\left\|\widehat{R}^{\dagger}-\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}O^{\dagger}_{t,S}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{CT^{3/4}}{(S-\lambda_{2}-h)^{\alpha-2}}+C\sqrt{\frac{S}{W}}.

Define
Z
as a standard normal random variable (i.e.,
\mathbf{E}\left[Z\right]=0
and
\mathrm{Var}\left(Z\right)=1
). For
\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}^{*}
has normal distribution with mean 0, the distribution of
\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}^{*}
coincide with
\left\|\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}^{*}\right\|_{2}Z.
Similarly,
\epsilon
has normal distribution with mean 0 and variance
\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right),
so the distribution of
\epsilon
coincide with
\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)}Z.
From this observation, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}^{*}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\epsilon\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\left\|\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}^{*}\right\|_{2}Z\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\left\|\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}Z\right)\right]\right|

(
\thechapter
.D.33)

\displaystyle\leq C\psi\left\|Z\right\|_{1}\left|\ \left\|\sum_{z=1}^{P}A_{z}^{*}\right\|_{2}-\left\|\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right\|_{2}\ \right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}\psi T^{3/4}}{(S-\lambda_{2}-h)^{\alpha-2}}+C_{1}\psi\sqrt{\frac{S}{W}}.

Choose
\psi=\log(T),S=\lfloor h+3\lambda_{2}\rfloor,W=\lfloor\sqrt{TS}\rfloor,
where
\lfloor x\rfloor
stands for the largest integer that is smaller than or equal to
x.
Since
S=o(T),

W<T
for sufficiently large
T,

\displaystyle\frac{\psi T^{3/4}}{(S-\lambda_{2}-h)^{\alpha-2}}\leq\frac{C\log(T)T^{3/4}}{T^{5/2}}=\frac{C\log(T)}{T^{7/4}},\hskip 10.00002pt\psi\sqrt{\frac{S}{W}}\leq C\log(T)\left(\frac{1}{T^{1-\kappa_{h}}}+\frac{1}{T^{1-\kappa_{2}}}\right)^{1/4},

\displaystyle\psi^{3}\sqrt{\frac{W}{T}}\leq C\log^{3}(T)\left(\frac{1}{T^{1-\kappa_{h}}}+\frac{1}{T^{1-\kappa_{2}}}\right)^{1/4}.

From (
\thechapter.D.19
), (
\thechapter.D.20
), (
\thechapter.D.27
), (
\thechapter.D.31
), (
\thechapter.D.32
), (
\thechapter.D.33
), we prove (
3.5
).
∎

We then prove the validity of the bootstrap algorithm
1
, which is summarized in Theorem
3.4
. Since Algorithm
1
samples joint normal random variables and constructs the bootstrap statistic as a linear combination of these sampled normal random variables, its validity actually relies on the fact that the variance of the bootstrap statistic in the bootstrap world closely approximates that of the estimator
\widehat{R},
which is proved below.

Assumption
\thechapter
.D.4
.

Define
\alpha
and
\kappa_{z},z=1,2,h
as those in Assumption
\thechapter.D.1
and Assumption
\thechapter.D.2
.
Assume that
\alpha>17
and
1/6>\kappa_{2}>\kappa_{1}>\frac{5}{2(\alpha-2)}
and
1/6>\kappa_{h}>0.
Suppose the bandwidth
\mathcal{K}_{T}\asymp T^{\kappa_{k}},
where
1/2-(\kappa_{2}\vee\kappa_{h})>\kappa_{k}>2(\kappa_{2}\vee\kappa_{h}).

Assumption
\thechapter.D.4
imposes a stronger condition than Assumption
\thechapter.D.1
and Assumption
\thechapter.D.2
. This trade-off is required for the consistent estimation of the variance of the estimator
\widehat{R}.

Proof of Theorem
3.4
.

Define “the expectation in the bootstrap world” as the conditional expectation conditional on
\mathbf{z}_{t},t=1,\cdots,T,
i.e.,

\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\cdot\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[\cdot\mid\mathbf{z}_{t},t=1,\cdots,T\ \right].

In the bootstrap world, since
e^{*}_{t},t=1,\cdots,T
has joint normal distribution,
\widehat{R}^{*}_{b}=\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}O_{t}e^{*}_{t}
also has normal distribution with

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*}_{b}\right]=\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}O_{t}\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[e^{*}_{t}\right]=0,

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*2}_{b}\right]=\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}O_{t_{1}}O_{t_{2}}\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[e^{*}_{t_{1}}e^{*}_{t_{2}}\right]=\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}O_{t_{1}}O_{t_{2}}K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right).

For

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)=\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\sum_{t=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}O_{t}^{\dagger}\right)^{2}\right]=\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right],

we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*2}_{b}\right]-\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\left(O_{t_{1}}O_{t_{2}}-O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\left(O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle+\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\left(1-K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\right)\left|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right]\right|.

For the first term,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\left(O_{t_{1}}O_{t_{2}}-O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\left\|O_{t_{1}}-O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle+\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\left\|O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|O_{t_{2}}-O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{T}(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(S-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\mathcal{U}}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=1}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\sum_{t_{1}=(t-\lambda_{2})\vee 1}^{t-\lambda_{1}}\sum_{s=1}^{h\wedge(t_{1}-1)}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{x}_{t-s}^{\top}\mathbf{x}_{t_{1}-s}\right]\right)\left(\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t,S}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[\mathbf{y}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{y}_{t_{1}}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(S-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M},

and therefore

\displaystyle\left\|P_{t,S}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=\left\|O_{t}^{\dagger}-O_{t,S}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}

(
\thechapter
.D.40)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}(S-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(S-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}.

Notice that

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\left(O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=0}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}K\left(\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}K\left(\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{2}+s}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{2}+s}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}.

For any
s\geq 0,

O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s}=O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}+P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1},
and
O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}
is independent of
O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}},
which makes

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right]\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\right]=0,

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s}O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\right].

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/4}+\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}.

From (
\thechapter.D.37
),

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/4}\leq\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4},

where
\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},-1}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\right]=0.

For any
j=0,1,\cdots
and
z=1,\cdots,T-s-\lambda_{1},
define

\displaystyle V_{z,j}=\sum_{t_{1}=T-s-z+1}^{T-s}O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right)

and
\mathcal{V}_{z,j}
as the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},e_{T-1},\cdots,e_{T-s-z+1-j}.
Then
V_{z,j}
is measurable in
\mathcal{V}_{z,j},

\mathcal{V}_{z,j}\subset\mathcal{V}_{z+1,j},
and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[V_{z+1,j}-V_{z,j}\mid\mathcal{V}_{z,j}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{T-z,s-1}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{T-s-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-s-z,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{T-s-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-s-z,j-1}\right]\right)\mid\mathcal{V}_{z,j}\right]

\displaystyle=O^{\dagger}_{T-z,s-1}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{T-s-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-s-z,j-1}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{T-s-z}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-s-z,j-1}\right]\right)=0.

Therefore,
V_{z,j}
forms a martingale, and according to Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al. 1972
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left\|O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-1}\right]\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}

\displaystyle=C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left\|O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}\left\|P^{\dagger}_{t_{1},j-1}-P^{\dagger}_{t_{1},j}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}.

From (
\thechapter.D.38
), (
\thechapter.D.39
), and (
\thechapter.D.40
), if
j\leq 2\lambda_{2}+h+1,

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left\|O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}\left\|P^{\dagger}_{t_{1},j-1}-P^{\dagger}_{t_{1},j}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}}.

On the other hand, if
j>2\lambda_{2}+h+1,

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left\|O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}\left\|P^{\dagger}_{t_{1},j-1}-P^{\dagger}_{t_{1},j}\right\|^{2}_{M/2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}(j-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}}\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{2\lambda_{2}+h+1}\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}}+\sum_{j=2\lambda_{2}+h+2}^{\infty}\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}(j-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}

(
\thechapter
.D.41)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=2\lambda_{2}+h+2}^{\infty}\frac{C\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}\lambda_{2}+C_{1}h}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{T}\lambda_{2}^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1}}(\lambda_{2}+h)^{\alpha-3}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}\lambda_{2}+C_{2}h}{\sqrt{T}}.

Since

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4},

where we define
\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,-1}\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\right].
For any
z=1,\cdots,T-s-\lambda_{1},
define

\displaystyle W_{z,j}=\sum_{t_{1}=T-s-z+1}^{T-s}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right),

and
\mathcal{W}_{z,j}
the
\sigma
-field generated by
e_{T},e_{T-1},\cdots,e_{T-z+1-j}.
Then
W_{z,j}
is measurable in
\mathcal{W}_{z,j},

\mathcal{W}_{z,j}\subset\mathcal{W}_{z+1,j},
and

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[W_{z+1,j}-W_{z,j}\mid\mathcal{W}_{z,j}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O_{T-s-z}^{\dagger}P_{T-z,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-z,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{T-s-z}^{\dagger}P_{T-z,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-z,j-1}\right]\right)\mid\mathcal{W}_{z,j}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}\left[O_{T-s-z}^{\dagger}P_{T-z,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-z,j-1}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{T-s-z}^{\dagger}P_{T-z,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{T-z,j-1}\right]=0,

so
W_{z,j}
forms a martingale. According to Theorem 1.1 of
Burkholder et al. 1972
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(
\thechapter
.D.42)

\displaystyle\leq C\sqrt{\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right\|^{2}_{M/4}}.

For any
j\in\mathbf{Z},

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/4}\leq 2\left\|O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}.

On the other hand, if
j>s,
we have

\displaystyle P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}

\displaystyle=O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}-O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}

(
\thechapter
.D.43)

\displaystyle=O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}-O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}+O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}-O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}

\displaystyle=P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}+O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}-O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1},

and
O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}-O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}
is measurable in
\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1},
which implies

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,j-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,j-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle+\left\|\left(O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}-O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right)\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right\|_{M/2}+\left\|O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}-O^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=2\left\|O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right\|_{M/2}+\left\|P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}-P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-s}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1},j-s-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle=2\left\|O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right\|_{M/2}+\left\|P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}-P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|P^{\dagger}_{t_{1},j-s-1}-P^{\dagger}_{t_{1},j-s}\right\|_{M/2}.

Suppose
s\leq 2\lambda_{2}+h+1.
In this case, if
j\leq 4\lambda_{2}+2h+2,
then from (
\thechapter.D.34
) and (
\thechapter.D.39
)

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C}{T},

which makes

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}}.

(
\thechapter
.D.44)

If
j>4\lambda_{2}+2h+2,
then
j-s-1>4\lambda_{2}+2h+2-(2\lambda_{2}+h+1)=2\lambda_{2}+h+1,
and (
\thechapter.D.40
) makes

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{T(j-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}

\displaystyle+\frac{C}{T(j-s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{T(j-s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}.

Applying this inequality to (
\thechapter.D.42
), we derive

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{T}(j-s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}.

Combine this inequality with (
\thechapter.D.44
) and take summation,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(
\thechapter
.D.45)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{4\lambda_{2}+2h+2}\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}}+\sum_{j=4\lambda_{2}+2h+3}^{\infty}\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}(j-s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=4\lambda_{2}+2h+3}^{\infty}\frac{C\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}\lambda_{2}+C_{1}h}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{T}\lambda_{2}^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(\lambda_{2}+h)^{\alpha-3}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}\lambda_{2}+C_{2}h}{\sqrt{T}}.

Equation (
\thechapter.D.45
) derives the moment bound for
\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}
under the setting
s\leq 2\lambda_{2}+h+1.
We next turn to the complementary case where

In this case, if

) and (
\thechapter.D.40
),

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}}{T(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M},

which, according to (
\thechapter.D.42
), makes

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}.

If
j>2s+1,
then
j-s-1>s>2\lambda_{2}+h+1.
We apply (
\thechapter.D.43
) and derive

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left\|O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right\|_{M/2}+\left\|P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}-P^{\dagger}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|P^{\dagger}_{t_{1},j-s-1}-P^{\dagger}_{t_{1},j-s}\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{T(j-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}

\displaystyle+\left(\left(\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{1/2+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}(j-s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{1/2+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}\right)\right).

Apply (
\thechapter.D.42
), it makes

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j}\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,j-1}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}(j-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}

\displaystyle+\left(\left(\frac{C}{(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{1+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}(j-s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{1/2+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}\right)\right).

From these observations,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}P_{t_{1}+s,s-1}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

(
\thechapter
.D.46)

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=0}^{2s+1}\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=2s+2}^{\infty}\frac{C}{\sqrt{T}(j-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=2s+2}^{\infty}\left(\left(\frac{C}{(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{1+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}(j-s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{T^{1/2+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(j-s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{1}s}{\sqrt{T}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}s\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{1}}{\sqrt{T}(2s+1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{C_{1}\sqrt{T}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(2s+1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-3}}T^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}

\displaystyle+\left(\left(\frac{C_{1}}{(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{C_{1}T^{1+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}(s+1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-1}}+\frac{T^{1/2+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s+1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-3}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C_{2}sT^{1/2+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{2}T^{1+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}\frac{T^{1/2+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s+1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-3}}.

This inequality applies the following facts:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{T^{1+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}},

\displaystyle\text{and}\hskip 10.00002pt\frac{1}{(s+1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{T^{1+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s+1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-3}}

for sufficiently large
T.
For
s>2\lambda_{2}+h+1
and
M>8,

\displaystyle\frac{sT^{1/2+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{T^{1/2+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-3}}+\frac{(1+h+\lambda_{2})T^{1/2+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{T^{1/2+2/M}}{\lambda_{2}^{\alpha-3}}+\frac{(1+h+\lambda_{2})T^{1/2+2/M}}{\lambda_{2}^{\alpha-2}}\leq\frac{C\lambda_{2}+Ch}{\sqrt{T}},

and

\displaystyle\frac{T^{3/2+4/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{4/M}}{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{2\alpha-5}}\leq\frac{T^{3/2+4/M}}{\lambda_{2}^{2\alpha-5}}\leq\frac{C\lambda_{2}+Ch}{\sqrt{T}}.

From (
\thechapter.D.41
), (
\thechapter.D.45
), and (
\thechapter.D.46
),

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}\leq\frac{C\lambda_{2}+Ch}{\sqrt{T}},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}K\left(\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{s=0}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}K\left(\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\frac{\lambda_{2}+h}{\sqrt{T}}\leq\frac{C_{1}\mathcal{K}_{T}(\lambda_{2}+h)}{\sqrt{T}}.

Since

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{2}+s}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{2}+s}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}\leq\frac{C\lambda_{2}+Ch}{\sqrt{T}},

we have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}K\left(\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\left\|\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left(O_{t_{2}+s}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{2}+s}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=1}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}K\left(\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\frac{C\lambda_{2}+Ch}{\sqrt{T}}\leq\frac{C_{1}\mathcal{K}_{T}(\lambda_{2}+h)}{\sqrt{T}},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\left(O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right]\right)\right\|_{M/4}\leq\frac{C\mathcal{K}_{T}(\lambda_{2}+h)}{\sqrt{T}}.

(
\thechapter
.D.47)

Since

\displaystyle\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T}\left(1-K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t_{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\right)\left|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=0}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}\left(1-K\left(\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\right)\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}\left(1-K\left(\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\right)\sum_{t_{2}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}+s}^{\dagger}\right]\right|

If
s\leq 2\lambda_{2}+h+1,
from (
\thechapter.D.34
),

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{2}+s}^{\dagger}\right]\right|\leq\left\|O_{t_{2}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|O_{t_{2}+s}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\leq\frac{C}{T}.

On the other hand, if
s>2\lambda_{2}+h+1,
since
\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right]
is independent of
O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger},
we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right]\right)\right]=\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\right]\mathbf{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right]\right)\right]=0,

and applying (
\thechapter.D.40
) leads to

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\left(O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right]\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left\|O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}\right\|_{M/2}\left\|O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}-\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\mid\mathcal{F}_{t_{1}+s,s-1}\right]\right\|_{M/2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{T(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\frac{CT^{2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}.

From Definition
1
,
\left|K^{\prime}(\cdot)\right|
is bounded by a constant, so for
0\leq s\leq\mathcal{K}_{T},

\displaystyle 1-K\left(\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)=K(0)-K\left(\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\leq\frac{Cs}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}.

From Assumption
\thechapter.D.4
,
2\lambda_{2}+h+1\leq\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor
for sufficiently large sample size
T,
where
\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor
denotes the largest integer that is smaller than or equal to
\mathcal{K}_{T}.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}\left(1-K\left(\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\right)\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\right]\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{s=0}^{2\lambda_{2}+h+1}\frac{Cs}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}+\sum_{s=2\lambda_{2}+h+2}^{\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor}\frac{Cs}{\mathcal{K}_{T}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\sum_{s=2\lambda_{2}+h+2}^{\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor}\frac{Cs}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\frac{T^{1+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor+1}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}\frac{C}{(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}+\sum_{s=\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor+1}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}\frac{CT^{1+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}.

Since

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{2\lambda_{2}+h+1}\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\leq\frac{C(\lambda_{2}+h)^{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}},

\displaystyle\sum_{s=2\lambda_{2}+h+2}^{\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor}\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=2\lambda_{2}+h+2}^{\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor}\frac{1}{\mathcal{K}_{T}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-1}}+\sum_{s=2\lambda_{2}+h+2}^{\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor}\frac{1+h+\lambda_{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\mathcal{K}_{T}\lambda_{2}^{\alpha-2}}+\frac{C(1+h+\lambda_{2})}{\mathcal{K}_{T}\lambda_{2}^{\alpha-1}}\leq\frac{C_{1}(\lambda_{2}+h)^{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}},

\displaystyle\sum_{s=2\lambda_{2}+h+2}^{\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor}\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\frac{T^{1+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=2\lambda_{2}+h+2}^{\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor}\frac{T^{1+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-3}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=2\lambda_{2}+h+2}^{\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor}\frac{(1+\lambda_{2})}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\frac{T^{1+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CT^{1+2/M}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}(\lambda_{2}+h)^{\alpha-4}}+\frac{(1+\lambda_{2})}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\frac{T^{1+2/M}}{(\lambda_{2}+h)^{\alpha-3}}\leq\frac{C(\lambda_{2}+h)^{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{s=\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor+1}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}\frac{1}{(s-1-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha}}\leq\frac{C}{(\mathcal{K}_{T}-h-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-1}}\leq\frac{C_{1}(\lambda_{2}+h)^{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}},

\displaystyle\sum_{s=\lfloor\mathcal{K}_{T}\rfloor+1}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}\frac{T^{1+2/M}(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})^{2/M}}{\sqrt{(\lambda_{2}-\lambda_{1})}(s-1-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-2}}\leq\frac{CT^{1+2/M}}{(\mathcal{K}_{T}-\lambda_{2})^{\alpha-3}}\leq\frac{C_{1}(\lambda_{2}+h)^{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}.

We have

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{T-1-\lambda_{1}}\left(1-K\left(\frac{s}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}\right)\right)\sum_{t_{1}=1+\lambda_{1}}^{T-s}\left|\mathbf{E}\left[O_{t_{1}}^{\dagger}O_{t_{1}+s}^{\dagger}\right]\right|\leq\frac{C(\lambda_{2}+h)^{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}.

(
\thechapter
.D.48)

From (
\thechapter.D.36
), (
\thechapter.D.47
), (
\thechapter.D.48
), and Assumption
\thechapter.D.4
,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*2}_{b}\right]-\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)\right\|_{M/4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C\mathcal{K}_{T}(\lambda_{2}+h)}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{C(\lambda_{2}+h)^{2}}{\mathcal{K}_{T}}

(
\thechapter
.D.49)

\displaystyle=O\left(T^{\kappa_{k}+(\kappa_{2}\vee\kappa_{h})-1/2}+T^{2(\kappa_{2}\vee\kappa_{h})-\kappa_{k}}\right).

Define
Z
as a standard normal random variable that is independent of
\mathbf{z}_{t},t=1,\cdots,T,
then

\displaystyle\mathbf{pr}^{*}\left(\widehat{R}^{*}_{b}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{pr}^{*}\left(\widehat{R}^{*}_{b}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x+1/\psi\right)+\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x+1/\psi\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\widehat{R}^{*}_{b}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\epsilon\right)\right]+\frac{C}{\psi}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sqrt{\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*2}_{b}\right]}Z\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[g_{\psi,x}\left(\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)}Z\right)\right]+\frac{C}{\psi}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\psi}+C\psi\left|\sqrt{\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*2}_{b}\right]}-\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)}\right|

and

\displaystyle\mathbf{pr}^{*}\left(\widehat{R}^{*}_{b}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{pr}^{*}\left(\widehat{R}^{*}_{b}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x-1/\psi\right)+\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x-1/\psi\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x\right)

\displaystyle\geq\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x-1/\psi}\left(\widehat{R}^{*}_{b}\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}\left[g_{\psi,x-1/\psi}\left(\epsilon\right)\right]-\frac{C}{\psi}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[g_{\psi,x-1/\psi}\left(\sqrt{\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*2}_{b}\right]}Z\right)\right]-\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[g_{\psi,x-1/\psi}\left(\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)}Z\right)\right]-\frac{C}{\psi}

\displaystyle\geq-\frac{C}{\psi}-C\psi\left|\sqrt{\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*2}_{b}\right]}-\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)}\right|.

These two inequalities make

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{pr}^{*}\left(\widehat{R}^{*}_{b}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C}{\psi}+C\psi\left|\sqrt{\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*2}_{b}\right]}-\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)}\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{C}{\psi}+C\psi\frac{\left|\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*2}_{b}\right]-\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)\right|}{\sqrt{\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*2}_{b}\right]}+\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)}}\leq\frac{C}{\psi}+C_{1}\psi\left|\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*2}_{b}\right]-\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)\right|.

From (
\thechapter.D.49
),
\left|\mathbf{E}^{*}\left[\widehat{R}^{*2}_{b}\right]-\mathrm{Var}\left(\widehat{R}^{\dagger}\right)\right|=O_{p}\left(T^{\kappa_{k}+(\kappa_{2}\vee\kappa_{h})-1/2}+T^{2(\kappa_{2}\vee\kappa_{h})-\kappa_{k}}\right).
Choose
\psi=\log(T),
we have

\displaystyle\sup_{x\in\mathbf{R}}\left|\mathbf{pr}^{*}\left(\widehat{R}^{*}_{b}\leq x\right)-\mathbf{pr}\left(\epsilon\leq x\right)\right|=o_{p}(1),

and we prove (
3.6
).

∎
